##新年双演说
country_event = {
	id = roc.01
	title = roc.01.title
	desc = roc.01.desc
	picture = GFX_ROC_Song_Jiang_event
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = roc.01.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = will_strong_song
		add_to_variable = { Song_strong = 1 }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = roc.01.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = will_strong_jiang
		add_to_variable = { Jiang_strong = 1 }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
		}
	}
}

##大萧条席卷广东
country_event = {
	id = roc.11
	title = roc.11.title
	desc = roc.11.desc
	picture = GFX_ROC_great_depression_event
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_from_array = { ROC_policy_unlock_card_array_true = 31 }
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array_true = 31 } }
				remove_from_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array_true = 31 }
				add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Economy_array_true = 0 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array_true = 31 } }
				remove_from_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array_true = 31 }
				add_to_array = { ROC_policy_select_card_Generic_array_true = 0 }
			}
			ROC_policy_card_modifier = yes
			country_event = {
				id = roc.12
				days = 10
			}
			country_event = {
				id = roc.14
				days = 15
			}
			country_event = {
				id = roc.24
				days = 30
			}
			set_variable = {
				ROC_Great_Depression_consumer_goods_factor = 0.3
			}
			set_variable = {
				ROC_Great_Depression_hcp_income_factor_monthly = -0.4
			}
			set_variable = {
				ROC_Great_Depression_production_speed_buildings_factor = -0.4
			}
			set_variable = {
				ROC_Great_Depression_industrial_capacity_factory = -0.2
			}
			set_variable = {
				ROC_Great_Depression_big_hand_num = 0
			}
		}
	}
	option = {#
		name = roc.11.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = roc.11.a.tt
		remove_ideas = ROC_trade_boom
		custom_effect_tooltip = remove_ROC_policy_card_name_31
		add_to_variable = {
			var = kmt_party_percent^2
			value = 0.05
			tooltip = ROC_RCKMT_influence_tooltip
		}
		ROC_factional_influence_calculation = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = will_strong_song
		#complete_national_focus = ROC_focus_jikan_kyou
		set_country_flag = ROC_GD_select_song_plan_flag
		add_political_power = 50
		add_to_variable = { Song_strong = 1 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = roc.11.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = roc.11.b.tt
		remove_ideas = ROC_trade_boom
		custom_effect_tooltip = remove_ROC_policy_card_name_31
		add_to_variable = {
			var = kmt_party_percent^2
			value = 0.05
			tooltip = ROC_RCKMT_influence_tooltip
		}
		ROC_factional_influence_calculation = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = will_strong_jiang
		#complete_national_focus = ROC_focus_hijou_shudan
		set_country_flag = ROC_GD_select_chiang_plan_flag
		add_command_power = 30
		add_to_variable = { Jiang_strong = 1 }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				add = 5
				has_country_flag = Jiang_jinguo_will_leninism
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = roc.11.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = roc.11.c.tt
		remove_ideas = ROC_trade_boom
		custom_effect_tooltip = remove_ROC_policy_card_name_31
		add_to_variable = {
			var = kmt_party_percent^3
			value = 0.05
			tooltip = ROC_Constitutionalists_influence_tooltip
		}
		#complete_national_focus = ROC_focus_shokku_ryouhou
		ROC_factional_influence_calculation = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

	}
	after = {
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = ROC_Great_Depression_modifier
		}
		activate_mission = ROC_Great_Depression_time_end
		activate_mission = ROC_Great_Depression_big_hand
	}
}

##铁证如山
country_event = {
	id = roc.13
	title = roc.13.title
	desc = roc.13.desc
	picture = GFX_ROC_Wang_Run_event
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			GXC = {
				country_event = {
					id = guangxi.001
					days = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = roc.13.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = roc.13.a.tt
		remove_from_array = { KMT_main_party_array = 7 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = RCAK_will_be_removed
		custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
		add_to_variable = {
			var = kmt_party_percent^5
			value = 0.025
			tooltip = ROC_Whampoa_influence_tooltip
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = kmt_party_percent^6
			value = 0.025
			tooltip = ROC_CNRS_influence_tooltip
		}
		ROC_factional_influence_calculation = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = remove_JAP_influence
		remove_from_array = { ROC_foreign_influence_array = JAP }
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_ideas = {
				ROC_influence_of_JAP_very_high
				ROC_influence_of_JAP_high
				ROC_influence_of_JAP_middle
				ROC_influence_of_JAP_low
				ROC_influence_of_JAP_very_low
			}
			ROC_foreign_influence_effect = yes
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = fascism
			name = ROC_CNRS_party
			long_name = ROC_CNRS_party_long
		}
		promote_character = {
			character = ROC_Herrscher_of_Sentience
			ideology = digital_fascism
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = roc.13.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = roc.13.b.tt
		remove_from_array = { KMT_main_party_array = 7 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = RCAK_will_be_removed
		custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
		add_to_variable = {
			var = kmt_party_percent^3
			value = 0.025
			tooltip = ROC_Constitutionalists_influence_tooltip
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = kmt_party_percent^4
			value = 0.025
			tooltip = ROC_CC_influence_tooltip
		}
		ROC_factional_influence_calculation = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = remove_JAP_influence
		remove_from_array = { ROC_foreign_influence_array = JAP }
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_ideas = {
				ROC_influence_of_JAP_very_high
				ROC_influence_of_JAP_high
				ROC_influence_of_JAP_middle
				ROC_influence_of_JAP_low
				ROC_influence_of_JAP_very_low
			}
			ROC_foreign_influence_effect = yes
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = fascism
			name = ROC_CNRS_party
			long_name = ROC_CNRS_party_long
		}
		promote_character = {

	title = roc.31.title
	desc = roc.31.desc
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {#同意
		name = roc.31.a
		ROC = {
			country_event = {
				id = roc.32
				days = 3
			}
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			send_equipment = {
				amount = 2500
				type = infantry_equipment
				target = FROM
			}
			send_equipment = {
				amount = 300
				target = FROM
				type = artillery_equipment
			}	
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment
				amount = -2500
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = artillery_equipment
				amount = -300
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 1.0
				OR = {
					check_variable = {
						ROC.num_equipment@infantry_equipment < 0
					}
					check_variable = {
						ROC.num_equipment@artillery_equipment < 0
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 0.5
				FROM = {
					has_war = yes
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				has_equipment = {
					infantry_equipment > 5000
				}
				has_equipment = {
					artillery_equipment > 500
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {#拒绝
		name = roc.31.b
		ROC = {
			country_event = {
				id = roc.33
				days = 3
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}

##ENG出局
country_event = {
	id = roc.85
	title = roc.85.title
	desc = roc.85.desc
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		ENG = { is_in_array = { ROOT.ROC_foreign_influence_array = THIS } }
		ENG = {
			or = {
				exists = no
				has_capitulated = yes
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
		}
		tag = ROC
	}
	option = {
		name = roc.85.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = remove_ENG_influence
		remove_from_array = { ROC_foreign_influence_array = ENG }
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_ideas = {
				ROC_influence_of_ENG_very_high
				ROC_influence_of_ENG_high
				ROC_influence_of_ENG_middle
				ROC_influence_of_ENG_low
				ROC_influence_of_ENG_very_low
			}
			ROC_foreign_influence_effect = yes
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = remove_somebody_out
		set_variable = { temp_influence = 0 }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					temp_influence < ENG.InfluenceWithROC
				}
			}
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = {
					type = industrial_complex
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { temp_influence = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
		}
	}
}

#统一日
country_event = {
	id = roc.94
	title = roc.94.title
	desc = roc.94.desc
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	picture = GFX_PRC_Mao_event
	option = {
		name = roc.94.a
		set_country_flag = ROC_Form_coalition_government
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = ROC_idea_integrate_the_ccp
			days = 365
		}
		PRC = {
			every_other_country = {
				limit = { is_subject_of = PREV }
				every_owned_state = {
					add_dynamic_modifier = {
						modifier = ROC_ccp_autonomous_region
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { is_chinese_country = yes }
					ROOT = {
						set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = PREV }
						CHI_puppet_released_tag_and_old_govern = yes
					}
				}
				else = {
					ROOT = {
						set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = PREV }
						puppet_released_tag_and_old_govern = yes
					}
				}
			}
			every_owned_state = {
				add_dynamic_modifier = {
					modifier = ROC_ccp_autonomous_region
				}
			}
			every_core_state = {
				add_core_of = ROOT
			}
			every_character = {
				limit = { is_general_advisor = no }
				set_nationality = ROOT
			}
			ROOT = { annex_country = { target = PREV transfer_troops = yes } }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			news_event = { id = roc_news.011 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
	}
}

country_event = {  ##邓择笙的演讲
	id = roc.175
	title = roc.175.title
	desc = roc.175.desc
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	picture = GFX_ROC_Deng_speech_event
	option = {
		name = roc.175.a
		if = {
			limit = { LOU = { is_subject_of = ROOT } }
			ROC_Deng_Yanda = { set_nationality = LOU }
			LOU = {
				every_possible_country = {
					limit = { has_character = JAP_shana }
					JAP_shana = { set_nationality = LOU }
				}
				promote_character = {
					character = JAP_shana
					ideology = Radical_socialism
				}
				set_country_leader_ideology = Three_Principles_of_the_People_c
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = communism
					name = JAP_KMT_party
					long_name = JAP_KMT_party_long
				}
				add_ideas = ROC_Deng_Yanda_idea
				set_cosmetic_tag = SHN
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { CHB = { is_subject_of = ROOT } }
			ROC_Deng_Yanda = { set_nationality = CHB }
			CHB = {
				every_possible_country = {
					limit = { has_character = JAP_shana }
					JAP_shana = { set_nationality = CHB }
				}
				promote_character = {
					character = JAP_shana
					ideology = Radical_socialism
				}
				set_country_leader_ideology = Three_Principles_of_the_People_c
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = communism
					name = JAP_KMT_party
					long_name = JAP_KMT_party_long
				}
				add_ideas = ROC_Deng_Yanda_idea
				set_cosmetic_tag = SHN
				if = {
					limit = { SGU = { is_subject_of = ROOT } }
					annex_country = { target = SGU transfer_troops = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SGU = { is_subject_of = ROOT } }
			ROC_Deng_Yanda = { set_nationality = SGU }
			SGU = {
				every_possible_country = {
					limit = { has_character = JAP_shana }
					JAP_shana = { set_nationality = SGU }
				}
				promote_character = {
					character = JAP_shana
					ideology = Radical_socialism
				}
				set_country_leader_ideology = Three_Principles_of_the_People_c
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = communism
					name = JAP_KMT_party
					long_name = JAP_KMT_party_long
				}
				add_ideas = ROC_Deng_Yanda_idea
				set_cosmetic_tag = SHN
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
	}
}

country_event = {  ##东京的春天
	id = roc.251
	title = roc.251.title
	desc = roc.251.desc
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	picture = GFX_PRC_Momo_and_Yuko_event
	option = {
		name = roc.251.a
		add_political_power = 10
		if = {
			limit = { LOU = { is_subject_of = ROOT } }
			LOU = {
				every_possible_country = {
					limit = { has_character = PRC_Chiyoda_Momo }
					PRC_Chiyoda_Momo = { set_nationality = LOU }
				}
				every_possible_country = {
					limit = { has_character = PRC_Yoshida_Yuko }
					PRC_Yoshida_Yuko = { set_nationality = LOU }
				}
				PRC_Chiyoda_Momo = {
					if = {
						limit = { can_be_country_leader = yes }
						ROOT = { promote_character = PRC_Chiyoda_Momo }
					}
					else = {
						add_country_leader_role = {
							country_leader = {
								desc = ""
								ideology = Three_Principles_of_the_People_c
								traits = {
									PRC_Red_Witch
								}
								expire = "1999.1.1.1"
								id = -1
							}
							promote_leader = yes
						}
					}
				}
				set_country_leader_ideology = Three_Principles_of_the_People_c
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = communism
					name = JAP_KMT_party
					long_name = JAP_KMT_party_long
				}
				add_ideas = PRC_Yoshida_Yuko_idea
				set_cosmetic_tag = SHN
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { CHB = { is_subject_of = ROOT } }
			CHB = {
				every_possible_country = {
					limit = { has_character = PRC_Chiyoda_Momo }
					PRC_Chiyoda_Momo = { set_nationality = CHB }
				}
				every_possible_country = {
					limit = { has_character = PRC_Yoshida_Yuko }
					PRC_Yoshida_Yuko = { set_nationality = CHB }
				}
				PRC_Chiyoda_Momo = {
					if = {
						limit = { can_be_country_leader = yes }
						ROOT = { promote_character = PRC_Chiyoda_Momo }
					}
					else = {
						add_country_leader_role = {
							country_leader = {
								desc = ""
								ideology = Three_Principles_of_the_People_c
								traits = {
									PRC_Red_Witch
								}
								expire = "1999.1.1.1"
								id = -1
							}
							promote_leader = yes
						}
					}
				}
				set_country_leader_ideology = Three_Principles_of_the_People_c
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = communism
					name = JAP_KMT_party
					long_name = JAP_KMT_party_long
				}
				add_ideas = PRC_Yoshida_Yuko_idea
				set_cosmetic_tag = SHN
				if = {
					limit = { SGU = { is_subject_of = ROOT } }
					annex_country = { target = SGU transfer_troops = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SGU = { is_subject_of = ROOT } }
			SGU = {
				every_possible_country = {
					limit = { has_character = PRC_Chiyoda_Momo }
					PRC_Chiyoda_Momo = { set_nationality = SGU }
				}
				every_possible_country = {
					limit = { has_character = PRC_Yoshida_Yuko }
					PRC_Yoshida_Yuko = { set_nationality = SGU }
				}
				PRC_Chiyoda_Momo = {
					if = {
						limit = { can_be_country_leader = yes }
						ROOT = { promote_character = PRC_Chiyoda_Momo }
					}
					else = {
						add_country_leader_role = {
							country_leader = {
								desc = ""
								ideology = Three_Principles_of_the_People_c
								traits = {
									PRC_Red_Witch
								}
								expire = "1999.1.1.1"
								id = -1
							}
							promote_leader = yes
						}
					}
				}
				set_country_leader_ideology = Three_Principles_of_the_People_c
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = communism
					name = JAP_KMT_party
					long_name = JAP_KMT_party_long
				}
				add_ideas = PRC_Yoshida_Yuko_idea
				set_cosmetic_tag = SHN
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = {
				id = roc.252
				days = 7
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
	}
}

	id = ROC_navy_trade.1
	title = ROC_navy_trade.1.t
	desc = ROC_navy_trade.1.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {#我们同意
		name = ROC_navy_trade.1.a
		add_to_variable = {
			var = ubs_save
			value = 100
			tooltip = UBS_SAVE_UP_100
		}
		UBS_bank_icon_frames = yes
		FROM = {
			country_event = {
				id = ROC_navy_trade.5
				days = 3
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				ROC = {
					has_war_with = JAP
				}
			factor = 0
			}
		}
	}
	option = {#不好意思，我们不卖

		FROM = {
			country_event = {
				id = ROC_navy_trade.4
				days = 3
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 3
		}
	}
}

#核心圈

country_event = {
	id = ROC_inner.1
	title = ROC_inner.1.t
	desc = ROC_inner.1.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_completed_focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_reorganize_COD
				}
			}
			complete_national_focus = {
				focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_reorganize_COD
				use_side_message = yes
				originator_name = PRC_ZhouEnlai
			}
			clr_country_flag = ROC_enlai_focus_in_progress_flag
			country_event = {
				id = ROC_inner.2
				days = 30
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_completed_focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_focus_on_administration
					has_completed_focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_focus_on_intelligence
				}
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ROC_inner.1
				days = 56
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_shine_effect_on_focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_focus_on_administration
				}
				complete_national_focus = {
					focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_focus_on_administration
					use_side_message = yes
					originator_name = PRC_ZhouEnlai
				}
				clr_country_flag = ROC_enlai_focus_2_in_progress_flag
				activate_shine_on_focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_shield_of_revolution
				set_country_flag = ROC_enlai_focus_4_in_progress_flag
			}
			else = {
				complete_national_focus = {
					focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_focus_on_intelligence
					use_side_message = yes
					originator_name = PRC_ZhouEnlai
				}
				clr_country_flag = ROC_enlai_focus_3_in_progress_flag
				activate_shine_on_focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_blade_of_revolution
				set_country_flag = ROC_enlai_focus_5_in_progress_flag
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_completed_focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_blade_of_revolution
					has_completed_focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_shield_of_revolution
				}
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ROC_inner.1
				days = 56
			}
			activate_shine_on_focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_purge_party_discipline
			set_country_flag = ROC_enlai_focus_6_in_progress_flag
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ROC_inner_zhou_adm_flag
				}
				complete_national_focus = {
					focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_shield_of_revolution
					use_side_message = yes
					originator_name = PRC_ZhouEnlai
				}
				clr_country_flag = ROC_enlai_focus_4_in_progress_flag
			}
			else = {
				complete_national_focus = {
					focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_blade_of_revolution
					use_side_message = yes
					originator_name = PRC_ZhouEnlai
				}
				clr_country_flag = ROC_enlai_focus_5_in_progress_flag
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_completed_focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_purge_party_discipline
				}
			}
				complete_national_focus = {
					focus = ROC_focus_inner_circle_purge_party_discipline
					use_side_message = yes
					originator_name = PRC_ZhouEnlai
				}
			clr_country_flag = ROC_enlai_focus_6_in_progress_flag
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ROC_inner.2
	title = ROC_inner.2.t

news_event = {
	id = roc_news.001
	title = roc_news.001.t
	picture = GFX_news_yuegui_war
	desc = {
		text = roc_news.001.desc
	}
	trigger = {
		wants_world_news = yes
	}
	major = no
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = roc_news.001.a
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { tag = FROM } }
				every_other_country = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { THIS.Chinese_Secondary_News_button_var = 2 }
							is_chinese_country = yes
						}
					}
					news_event = { id = roc_news.001 }
				}
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			tag = ROC
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = roc_news.001.b
		trigger = {
			tag = GXC
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = roc_news.001.c
		trigger = {
			tag = CHI
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = roc_news.001.d
		trigger = {
			tag = PRC
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = roc_news.001.e
		trigger = {
			not = {
				or = {
					tag = ROC
					tag = GXC
					tag = CHI
					tag = PRC
				}
			}
		}
